# Graph Report - PRISM-prescript  (2026-09-28)

## Corpus Check
- 2508 files · ~4,791,408 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 44083 nodes · 80454 edges · 1757 communities (1361 shown, 396 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 94% EXTRACTED · 6% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4972 edges (avg confidence: 0.59)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `1026a7fc`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
